13"></path><path d="M12 6l0 13"></path><path d="M21 6l0 13"></path></svg>Description</h2><div class="prose prose-slate dark:prose-invert max-w-none section-overview"><div class="markdown-content  "><h1 id="️-langchain">🦜️🔗 LangChain</h1>
<p><a href="https://pypi.org/project/langchain/#history"><img src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langchain?label=%20" alt="PyPI - Version"></a>
<a href="https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT"><img src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/l/langchain" alt="PyPI - License"></a>
<a href="https://pypistats.org/packages/langchain"><img src="https://img.shields.io/pepy/dt/langchain" alt="PyPI - Downloads"></a>
<a href="https://x.com/langchain"><img src="https://img.shields.io/twitter/url/https/twitter.com/langchain.svg?style=social&#x26;label=Follow%20%40LangChain" alt="Twitter"></a></p>
<p>Looking for the JS/TS version? Check out <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchainjs">LangChain.js</a>.</p>
<p>To help you ship LangChain apps to production faster, check out <a href="https://www.langchain.com/langsmith">LangSmith</a>.
<a href="https://www.langchain.com/langsmith">LangSmith</a> is a unified developer platform for building, testing, and monitoring LLM applications.</p>
<h2 id="quick-install">Quick Install</h2>
<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">pip</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> install</span><span style="color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> langchain</span></span></code></pre>
<h2 id="-what-is-this">🤔 What is this?</h2>
<p>LangChain is the easiest way to start building agents and applications powered by LLMs. With under 10 lines of code, you can connect to OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/integrations/providers/overview">more</a>. LangChain provides a pre-built agent architecture and model integrations to help you get started quickly and seamlessly incorporate LLMs into your agents and applications.</p>
<p>We recommend you use LangChain if you want to quickly build agents and autonomous applications. Use <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/overview">LangGraph</a>, our low-level agent orchestration framework and runtime, when you have more advanced needs that require a combination of deterministic and agentic workflows, heavy customization, and carefully controlled latency.</p>
<p>LangChain <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/agents">agents</a> are built on top of LangGraph in order to provide durable execution, streaming, human-in-the-loop, persistence, and more. (You do not need to know LangGraph for basic LangChain agent usage.)</p>
<h2 id="-documentation">📖 Documentation</h2>
<p>For full documentation, see the <a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langchain/langchain/">API reference</a>. For conceptual guides, tutorials, and examples on using LangChain, see the <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview">LangChain Docs</a>. You can also chat with the docs using <a href="https://chat.langchain.com">Chat LangChain</a>.</p>
<h2 id="-releases--versioning">📕 Releases &#x26; Versioning</h2>
<p>See our <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/release-policy">Releases</a> and <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/versioning">Versioning</a> policies.</p>
<h2 id="-contributing">💁 Contributing</h2>
<p>As an open-source project in a rapidly developing field, we are extremely open to contributions, whether it be in the form of a new feature, improved infrastructure, or better documentation.</p>
<p>For detailed information on how to contribute, see the <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/contributing/overview">Contributing Guide</a>.</p></div></div></section><section langchain\n\n\u003e Python package\n\n📖 [View in docs](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langchain)\n\n# 🦜️🔗 LangChain\n\n[![PyPI - Version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langchain?label=%20)](https://pypi.org/project/langchain/#history)\n[![PyPI - License](https://img.shields.io/pypi/l/langchain)](https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT)\n[![PyPI - Downloads](https://img.shields.io/pepy/dt/langchain)](https://pypistats.org/packages/langchain)\n[![Twitter](https://img.shields.io/twitter/url/https/twitter.com/langchain.svg?style=social\u0026label=Follow%20%40LangChain)](https://x.com/langchain)\n\nLooking for the JS/TS version? Check out [LangChain.js](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchainjs).\n\nTo help you ship LangChain apps to production faster, check out [LangSmith](https://www.langchain.com/langsmith).\n[LangSmith](https://www.langchain.com/langsmith) is a unified developer platform for building, testing, and monitoring LLM applications.\n\n## Quick Install\n\n```bash\npip install langchain\n```\n\n## 🤔 What is this?\n\nLangChain is the easiest way to start building agents and applications powered by LLMs. With under 10 lines of code, you can connect to OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and [more](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/integrations/providers/overview). LangChain provides a pre-built agent architecture and model integrations to help you get started quickly and seamlessly incorporate LLMs into your agents and applications.\n\nWe recommend you use LangChain if you want to quickly build agents and autonomous applications. Use [LangGraph](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/overview), our low-level agent orchestration framework and runtime, when you have more advanced needs that require a combination of deterministic and agentic workflows, heavy customization, and carefully controlled latency.\n\nLangChain [agents](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/agents) are built on top of LangGraph in order to provide durable execution, streaming, human-in-the-loop, persistence, and more. (You do not need to know LangGraph for basic LangChain agent usage.)\n\n## 📖 Documentation\n\nFor full documentation, see the [API reference](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langchain/langchain/). For conceptual guides, tutorials, and examples on using LangChain, see the [LangChain Docs](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview). You can also chat with the docs using [Chat LangChain](https://chat.langchain.com).\n\n## 📕 Releases \u0026 Versioning\n\nSee our [Releases](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/release-policy) and [Versioning](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/versioning) policies.\n\n## 💁 Contributing\n\nAs an open-source project in a rapidly developing field, we are extremely open to contributions, whether it be in the form of a new feature, improved infrastructure, or better documentation.\n\nFor detailed information on how to contribute, see the [Contributing Guide](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/contributing/overview).\n\n## Classes\n\n- [`StructuredOutputError`](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langchain/agents/structured_output/StructuredOutputError)\n- LangChain\u003c/h1\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://pypi.org/project/langchain/#history\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langchain?label=%20\" alt=\"PyPI - Version\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003ca href=\"https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://img.shields.io/pypi/l/langchain\" alt=\"PyPI - License\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003ca href=\"https://pypistats.org/packages/langchain\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://img.shields.io/pepy/dt/langchain\" alt=\"PyPI - Downloads\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003ca href=\"https://x.com/langchain\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://img.shields.io/twitter/url/https/twitter.com/langchain.svg?style=social\u0026#x26;label=Follow%20%40LangChain\" alt=\"Twitter\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eLooking for the JS/TS version? Check out \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchainjs\"\u003eLangChain.js\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eTo help you ship LangChain apps to production faster, check out \u003ca href=\"https://www.langchain.com/langsmith\"\u003eLangSmith\u003c/a\u003e.\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.langchain.com/langsmith\"\u003eLangSmith\u003c/a\u003e is a unified developer platform for building, testing, and monitoring LLM applications.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"quick-install\"\u003eQuick Install\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cpre class=\"shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark\" style=\"background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8\" tabindex=\"0\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e\u003cspan class=\"line\"\u003e\u003cspan style=\"color:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0\"\u003epip\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan style=\"color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF\"\u003e install\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan style=\"color:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF\"\u003e langchain\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"-what-is-this\"\u003e🤔 What is this?\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eLangChain is the easiest way to start building agents and applications powered by LLMs. With under 10 lines of code, you can connect to OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/integrations/providers/overview\"\u003emore\u003c/a\u003e. LangChain provides a pre-built agent architecture and model integrations to help you get started quickly and seamlessly incorporate LLMs into your agents and applications.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eWe recommend you use LangChain if you want to quickly build agents and autonomous applications. Use \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/overview\"\u003eLangGraph\u003c/a\u003e, our low-level agent orchestration framework and runtime, when you have more advanced needs that require a combination of deterministic and agentic workflows, heavy customization, and carefully controlled latency.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eLangChain \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/agents\"\u003eagents\u003c/a\u003e are built on top of LangGraph in order to provide durable execution, streaming, human-in-the-loop, persistence, and more. (You do not need to know LangGraph for basic LangChain agent usage.)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"-documentation\"\u003e📖 Documentation\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eFor full documentation, see the \u003ca href=\"https://reference.langchain.com/python/langchain/langchain/\"\u003eAPI reference\u003c/a\u003e. For conceptual guides, tutorials, and examples on using LangChain, see the \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview\"\u003eLangChain Docs\u003c/a\u003e. You can also chat with the docs using \u003ca href=\"https://chat.langchain.com\"\u003eChat LangChain\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"-releases--versioning\"\u003e📕 Releases \u0026#x26; Versioning\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eSee our \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/release-policy\"\u003eReleases\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/versioning\"\u003eVersioning\u003c/a\u003e policies.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"-contributing\"\u003e💁 Contributing\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eAs an open-source project in a rapidly developing field, we are extremely open to contributions, whether it be in the form of a new feature, improved infrastructure, or better documentation.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eFor detailed information on how to contribute, see the \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/contributing/overview\"\u003eContributing Guide\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"a9:Tb51,"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"# 🦜️🔗 LangChain\n\n[![PyPI - Version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langchain?label=%20)](https://pypi.org/project/langchain/#history)\n[![PyPI - License](https://img.shields.io/pypi/l/langchain)](https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT)\n[![PyPI - Downloads](https://img.shields.io/pepy/dt/langchain)](https://pypistats.org/packages/langchain)\n[![Twitter](https://img.shields.io/twitter/url/https/twitter.com/langchain.svg?style=social\u0026label=Follow%20%40LangChain)](https://x.com/langchain)\n\nLooking for the JS/TS version? Check out [LangChain.js](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchainjs).\n\nTo help you ship LangChain apps to production faster, check out [LangSmith](https://www.langchain.com/langsmith).\n[LangSmith](https://www.langchain.com/langsmith) is a unified developer platform for building, testing, and monitoring LLM applications.\n\n## Quick Install\n\n```bash\npip install langchain\n```\n\n## 🤔 What is this?\n\nLangChain is the easiest way to start building agents and applications powered by LLMs. With under 10 lines of code, you can connect to OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and [more](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/integrations/providers/overview). LangChain provides a pre-built agent architecture and model integrations to help you get started quickly and seamlessly incorporate LLMs into your agents and applications.\n\nWe recommend you use LangChain if you want to quickly build agents and autonomous applications. Use [LangGraph](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/overview), our low-level agent orchestration framework and runtime, when you have more advanced needs that require a combination of deterministic and agentic workflows, heavy customization, and carefully controlled latency.\n\nLangChain [agents](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/agents) are built on top of LangGraph in order to provide durable execution, streaming, human-in-the-loop, persistence, and more. (You do not need to know LangGraph for basic LangChain agent usage.)\n\n## 📖 Documentation\n\nFor full documentation, see the [API reference](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langchain/langchain/). For conceptual guides, tutorials, and examples on using LangChain, see the [LangChain Docs](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview). You can also chat with the docs using [Chat LangChain](https://chat.langchain.com).\n\n## 📕 Releases \u0026 Versioning\n\nSee our [Releases](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/release-policy) and [Versioning](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/versioning) policies.\n\n## 💁 Contributing\n\nAs an open-source project in a rapidly developing field, we are extremely open to contributions, whether it be in the form of a new feature, improved infrastructure, or better documentation.\n\nFor detailed information on how to contribute, see the [Contributing Guide](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/contributing/overview)."])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"2d:[\"$\",\"$La7\",null,{\"html\":\"$a8\",\"rawContent\":\"$a9\",\"className\":\"\",\"compact\":false}]\n1e:[[\"$\",\"meta\",\"0\",{\"charSet\":\"utf-8\"}],[\"$\",\"meta\",\"1\",{\"name\":\"viewport\",\"content\":\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\"}]]\n1c:null\n"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"20:[[\"$\",\"title\",\"0\",{\"children\":\"langchain | LangChain Reference\"}],[\"$\",\"meta\",\"1\",{\"name\":\"description\",\"content\":\"Python API reference for langchain.
